cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(nbody_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(nbody nbody.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(nbody PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(nbody m)
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The benchmark computes all pairwise forces in double precision, then updates positions. I’ll keep the state on the GPU, reuse positions through shared-memory tiles, and write each step into a separate position buffer. That preserves the order of force accumulation and prevents bodies from seeing partially updated positions.

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }

+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Explicit rounding keeps the velocity update separate from integration.
+// Fused operations elsewhere preserve the original optimized CPU arithmetic.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(double dx, double dy, double dz) {
+    return __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+}
+
+template<int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t stride, int n) {
+    __shared__ double sx[TILE_SIZE], sy[TILE_SIZE], sz[TILE_SIZE];
+    const size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const bool active = i < size_t(n);
+    const double x = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < size_t(n); base += TILE_SIZE) {
+        const int count = int(size_t(n) - base < TILE_SIZE ? size_t(n) - base : TILE_SIZE);
+        for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < count; k += BLOCK_SIZE) {
+            sx[k] = positions[base + k];
+            sy[k] = positions[stride + base + k];
+            sz[k] = positions[2 * stride + base + k];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (active) {
+            // Keep the j accumulation order, including the zero self force.
+            #pragma unroll 8
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const double dx = sx[j] - x;
+                const double dy = sy[j] - y;
+                const double dz = sz[j] - z;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz));
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive lanes also participate before the next tile overwrites it.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

+        const double vz = velocities[2 * stride + i] + DT * fz;
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;

+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, x);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, y);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, z);

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n(int(bodies.size())), stride((size_t(n) + 31) / 32 * 32) {
+        // A GPU is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU path.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "creating stream");
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage, 9 * stride * sizeof(double)), "allocating body state");
+        positions = storage;
+        velocities = storage + 3 * stride;
+        nextPositions = storage + 6 * stride;
+        std::vector<double> initial(6 * stride, 0.0);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            initial[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            initial[stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            initial[2 * stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            initial[3 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            initial[4 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            initial[5 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(storage, initial.data(), initial.size() * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading body state");
+        // An even graph length leaves the position buffers in their initial roles.
+        // Bounded batches avoid building a graph proportional to the step count.
+        graphSteps = steps > 1 ? (steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32) : 0;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal), "starting graph capture");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps; ++step) launchStep();
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "ending graph capture");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executableGraph, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0), "instantiating graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroying captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (executableGraph) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executableGraph);
+        if (storage) cudaFree(storage);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        int remaining = steps;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            for (; remaining >= graphSteps; remaining -= graphSteps)
+                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executableGraph, stream), "launching simulation graph");
+        }
+        for (; remaining > 0; --remaining) launchStep();
+        // Include completion of all GPU work in the reported simulation time.
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "running simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        std::vector<double> final(6 * stride);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data(), positions, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data() + 3 * stride, velocities, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading velocities");
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(final[i], final[stride + i], final[2 * stride + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(final[3 * stride + i], final[4 * stride + i], final[5 * stride + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    double totalEnergy() const {
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        double* deviceEnergies = nullptr;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double)), "allocating energy output");
+        energyPerBody<<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, velocities, deviceEnergies, stride, n);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching energy kernel");
+        std::vector<double> energies(n);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "computing energy");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(energies.data(), deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading energies");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceEnergies), "freeing energy output");
+        return kahan_sum(energies);
+    }
+
+private:
+    void launchStep() {
+        if (n < 8192) {
+            advanceBodies<32><<<(size_t(n) + 31) / 32, 32, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<128><<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching body kernel");
+        std::swap(positions, nextPositions);
+    }
+
+    int n;
+    size_t stride;
+    double* storage = nullptr;
+    double* positions = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions = nullptr;
+    double* velocities = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executableGraph = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps = 0;
+};
+

     for (const auto& body : bodies) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Explicit rounding keeps the velocity update separate from integration.
+// Fused operations elsewhere preserve the original optimized CPU arithmetic.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(double dx, double dy, double dz) {
+    return __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+}
+
+template<int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t stride, int n) {
+    __shared__ double sx[TILE_SIZE], sy[TILE_SIZE], sz[TILE_SIZE];
+    const size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const bool active = i < size_t(n);
+    const double x = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < size_t(n); base += TILE_SIZE) {
+        const int count = int(size_t(n) - base < TILE_SIZE ? size_t(n) - base : TILE_SIZE);
+        for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < count; k += BLOCK_SIZE) {
+            sx[k] = positions[base + k];
+            sy[k] = positions[stride + base + k];
+            sz[k] = positions[2 * stride + base + k];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (active) {
+            // Keep the j accumulation order, including the zero self force.
+            #pragma unroll 8
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const double dx = sx[j] - x;
+                const double dy = sy[j] - y;
+                const double dz = sz[j] - z;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz));
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        // Inactive lanes also participate before the next tile overwrites it.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
-}

+        const double vy = velocities[stride + i] + DT * fy;
+        const double vz = velocities[2 * stride + i] + DT * fz;
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;

+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, x);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, y);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, z);

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n(int(bodies.size())), stride((size_t(n) + 31) / 32 * 32) {
+        // A GPU is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU path.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "creating stream");
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage, 9 * stride * sizeof(double)), "allocating body state");
+        positions = storage;
+        velocities = storage + 3 * stride;
+        nextPositions = storage + 6 * stride;
+        std::vector<double> initial(6 * stride, 0.0);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            initial[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            initial[stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            initial[2 * stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            initial[3 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            initial[4 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            initial[5 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(storage, initial.data(), initial.size() * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading body state");
+        // An even graph length leaves the position buffers in their initial roles.
+        // Bounded batches avoid building a graph proportional to the step count.
+        graphSteps = steps > 1 ? (steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32) : 0;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal), "starting graph capture");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps; ++step) launchStep();
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "ending graph capture");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executableGraph, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0), "instantiating graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroying captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (executableGraph) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executableGraph);
+        if (storage) cudaFree(storage);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        int remaining = steps;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            for (; remaining >= graphSteps; remaining -= graphSteps)
+                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executableGraph, stream), "launching simulation graph");
+        }
+        for (; remaining > 0; --remaining) launchStep();
+        // Include completion of all GPU work in the reported simulation time.
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "running simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        std::vector<double> final(6 * stride);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data(), positions, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data() + 3 * stride, velocities, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading velocities");
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(final[i], final[stride + i], final[2 * stride + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(final[3 * stride + i], final[4 * stride + i], final[5 * stride + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    double totalEnergy() const {
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        double* deviceEnergies = nullptr;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double)), "allocating energy output");
+        energyPerBody<<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, velocities, deviceEnergies, stride, n);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching energy kernel");
+        std::vector<double> energies(n);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "computing energy");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(energies.data(), deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading energies");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceEnergies), "freeing energy output");
+        return kahan_sum(energies);
+    }
+
+private:
+    void launchStep() {
+        if (n < 8192) {
+            advanceBodies<32><<<(size_t(n) + 31) / 32, 32, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<128><<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching body kernel");
+        std::swap(positions, nextPositions);
+    }
+
+    int n;
+    size_t stride;
+    double* storage = nullptr;
+    double* positions = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions = nullptr;
+    double* velocities = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executableGraph = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps = 0;
+};
+

     for (const auto& body : bodies) {

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    CudaSimulation simulation(bodies, numSteps);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    simulation.run(numSteps);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    simulation.download(bodies);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = simulation.totalEnergy();
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n(int(bodies.size())), stride((size_t(n) + 31) / 32 * 32) {
+        // A GPU is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU path.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "creating stream");
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage, 9 * stride * sizeof(double)), "allocating body state");
+        positions = storage;
+        velocities = storage + 3 * stride;
+        nextPositions = storage + 6 * stride;
+        std::vector<double> initial(6 * stride, 0.0);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            initial[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            initial[stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            initial[2 * stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            initial[3 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            initial[4 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            initial[5 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(storage, initial.data(), initial.size() * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading body state");
+        // An even graph length leaves the position buffers in their initial roles.
+        // Bounded batches avoid building a graph proportional to the step count.
+        graphSteps = steps > 1 ? (steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32) : 0;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal), "starting graph capture");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps; ++step) launchStep();
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "ending graph capture");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executableGraph, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0), "instantiating graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroying captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (executableGraph) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executableGraph);
+        if (storage) cudaFree(storage);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        int remaining = steps;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            for (; remaining >= graphSteps; remaining -= graphSteps)
+                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executableGraph, stream), "launching simulation graph");
+        }
+        for (; remaining > 0; --remaining) launchStep();
+        // Include completion of all GPU work in the reported simulation time.
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "running simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        std::vector<double> final(6 * stride);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data(), positions, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data() + 3 * stride, velocities, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading velocities");
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(final[i], final[stride + i], final[2 * stride + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(final[3 * stride + i], final[4 * stride + i], final[5 * stride + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    double totalEnergy() const {
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        double* deviceEnergies = nullptr;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double)), "allocating energy output");
+        energyPerBody<<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, velocities, deviceEnergies, stride, n);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching energy kernel");
+        std::vector<double> energies(n);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "computing energy");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(energies.data(), deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading energies");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceEnergies), "freeing energy output");
+        return kahan_sum(energies);
+    }
+
+private:
+    void launchStep() {
+        if (n < 8192) {
+            advanceBodies<32><<<(size_t(n) + 31) / 32, 32, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<128><<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching body kernel");
+        std::swap(positions, nextPositions);
+    }
+
+    int n;
+    size_t stride;
+    double* storage = nullptr;
+    double* positions = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions = nullptr;
+    double* velocities = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executableGraph = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps = 0;
+};
+

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Explicit rounding keeps the velocity update separate from integration.
+// Fused operations elsewhere preserve the original optimized CPU arithmetic.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(double dx, double dy, double dz) {
+    return __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+}
+
+template<int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t stride, int n) {
+    __shared__ double sx[TILE_SIZE], sy[TILE_SIZE], sz[TILE_SIZE];
+    const size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const bool active = i < size_t(n);
+    const double x = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < size_t(n); base += TILE_SIZE) {
+        const int count = int(size_t(n) - base < TILE_SIZE ? size_t(n) - base : TILE_SIZE);
+        for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < count; k += BLOCK_SIZE) {
+            sx[k] = positions[base + k];
+            sy[k] = positions[stride + base + k];
+            sz[k] = positions[2 * stride + base + k];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (active) {
+            // Keep the j accumulation order, including the zero self force.
+            #pragma unroll 8
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const double dx = sx[j] - x;
+                const double dy = sy[j] - y;
+                const double dz = sz[j] - z;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz));
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
+        }
+        // Inactive lanes also participate before the next tile overwrites it.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = velocities[i] + DT * fx;
+        const double vy = velocities[stride + i] + DT * fy;
+        const double vz = velocities[2 * stride + i] + DT * fz;
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // Every force reads the old positions; the next step reads this buffer.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, x);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, y);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, z);
+    }
 }

+public:

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Explicit rounding keeps the velocity update separate from integration.
+// Fused operations elsewhere preserve the original optimized CPU arithmetic.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(double dx, double dy, double dz) {
+    return __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+}
+
+template<int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t stride, int n) {
+    __shared__ double sx[TILE_SIZE], sy[TILE_SIZE], sz[TILE_SIZE];
+    const size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const bool active = i < size_t(n);
+    const double x = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < size_t(n); base += TILE_SIZE) {
+        const int count = int(size_t(n) - base < TILE_SIZE ? size_t(n) - base : TILE_SIZE);
+        for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < count; k += BLOCK_SIZE) {
+            sx[k] = positions[base + k];
+            sy[k] = positions[stride + base + k];
+            sz[k] = positions[2 * stride + base + k];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (active) {
+            // Keep the j accumulation order, including the zero self force.
+            #pragma unroll 8
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const double dx = sx[j] - x;
+                const double dy = sy[j] - y;
+                const double dz = sz[j] - z;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz));
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
+        }
+        // Inactive lanes also participate before the next tile overwrites it.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = velocities[i] + DT * fx;
+        const double vy = velocities[stride + i] + DT * fy;
+        const double vz = velocities[2 * stride + i] + DT * fz;
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // Every force reads the old positions; the next step reads this buffer.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, x);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, y);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, z);
+    }
 }

+__global__ void energyPerBody(const double* __restrict__ positions,

+    if (active) {

+        energy = 0.5 * __fma_rn(vz, vz, __fma_rn(vx, vx, vy * vy));

-

-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n(int(bodies.size())), stride((size_t(n) + 31) / 32 * 32) {
+        // A GPU is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU path.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "creating stream");
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage, 9 * stride * sizeof(double)), "allocating body state");
+        positions = storage;
+        velocities = storage + 3 * stride;
+        nextPositions = storage + 6 * stride;
+        std::vector<double> initial(6 * stride, 0.0);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            initial[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            initial[stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            initial[2 * stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            initial[3 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            initial[4 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            initial[5 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(storage, initial.data(), initial.size() * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading body state");
+        // An even graph length leaves the position buffers in their initial roles.
+        // Bounded batches avoid building a graph proportional to the step count.
+        graphSteps = steps > 1 ? (steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32) : 0;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal), "starting graph capture");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps; ++step) launchStep();
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "ending graph capture");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executableGraph, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0), "instantiating graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroying captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (executableGraph) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executableGraph);
+        if (storage) cudaFree(storage);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        int remaining = steps;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            for (; remaining >= graphSteps; remaining -= graphSteps)
+                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executableGraph, stream), "launching simulation graph");
+        }
+        for (; remaining > 0; --remaining) launchStep();
+        // Include completion of all GPU work in the reported simulation time.
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "running simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        std::vector<double> final(6 * stride);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data(), positions, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data() + 3 * stride, velocities, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading velocities");
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(final[i], final[stride + i], final[2 * stride + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(final[3 * stride + i], final[4 * stride + i], final[5 * stride + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    double totalEnergy() const {
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        double* deviceEnergies = nullptr;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double)), "allocating energy output");
+        energyPerBody<<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, velocities, deviceEnergies, stride, n);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching energy kernel");
+        std::vector<double> energies(n);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "computing energy");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(energies.data(), deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading energies");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceEnergies), "freeing energy output");
+        return kahan_sum(energies);
+    }
+
+private:
+    void launchStep() {
+        if (n < 8192) {
+            advanceBodies<32><<<(size_t(n) + 31) / 32, 32, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<128><<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching body kernel");
+        std::swap(positions, nextPositions);
+    }
+
+    int n;
+    size_t stride;
+    double* storage = nullptr;
+    double* positions = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions = nullptr;
+    double* velocities = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executableGraph = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps = 0;
+};
+

     for (const auto& body : bodies) {

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n(int(bodies.size())), stride((size_t(n) + 31) / 32 * 32) {
+        // A GPU is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU path.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "creating stream");
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage, 9 * stride * sizeof(double)), "allocating body state");
+        positions = storage;
+        velocities = storage + 3 * stride;
+        nextPositions = storage + 6 * stride;
+        std::vector<double> initial(6 * stride, 0.0);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            initial[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            initial[stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            initial[2 * stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            initial[3 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            initial[4 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            initial[5 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(storage, initial.data(), initial.size() * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading body state");
+        // An even graph length leaves the position buffers in their initial roles.
+        // Bounded batches avoid building a graph proportional to the step count.
+        graphSteps = steps > 1 ? (steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32) : 0;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal), "starting graph capture");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps; ++step) launchStep();
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "ending graph capture");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executableGraph, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0), "instantiating graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroying captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (executableGraph) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executableGraph);
+        if (storage) cudaFree(storage);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        int remaining = steps;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            for (; remaining >= graphSteps; remaining -= graphSteps)
+                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executableGraph, stream), "launching simulation graph");
+        }
+        for (; remaining > 0; --remaining) launchStep();
+        // Include completion of all GPU work in the reported simulation time.
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "running simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        std::vector<double> final(6 * stride);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data(), positions, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data() + 3 * stride, velocities, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading velocities");
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(final[i], final[stride + i], final[2 * stride + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(final[3 * stride + i], final[4 * stride + i], final[5 * stride + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    double totalEnergy() const {
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        double* deviceEnergies = nullptr;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double)), "allocating energy output");
+        energyPerBody<<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, velocities, deviceEnergies, stride, n);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching energy kernel");
+        std::vector<double> energies(n);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "computing energy");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(energies.data(), deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading energies");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceEnergies), "freeing energy output");
+        return kahan_sum(energies);
+    }
+
+private:
+    void launchStep() {
+        if (n < 8192) {
+            advanceBodies<32><<<(size_t(n) + 31) / 32, 32, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<128><<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching body kernel");
+        std::swap(positions, nextPositions);
+    }
+
+    int n;
+    size_t stride;
+    double* storage = nullptr;
+    double* positions = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions = nullptr;
+    double* velocities = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executableGraph = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps = 0;
+};
+

+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Explicit rounding keeps the velocity update separate from integration.
+// Fused operations elsewhere preserve the original optimized CPU arithmetic.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(double dx, double dy, double dz) {
+    return __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+}
+
+template<int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t stride, int n) {
+    __shared__ double sx[TILE_SIZE], sy[TILE_SIZE], sz[TILE_SIZE];
+    const size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const bool active = i < size_t(n);
+    const double x = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < size_t(n); base += TILE_SIZE) {
+        const int count = int(size_t(n) - base < TILE_SIZE ? size_t(n) - base : TILE_SIZE);
+        for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < count; k += BLOCK_SIZE) {
+            sx[k] = positions[base + k];
+            sy[k] = positions[stride + base + k];
+            sz[k] = positions[2 * stride + base + k];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (active) {
+            // Keep the j accumulation order, including the zero self force.
+            #pragma unroll 8
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const double dx = sx[j] - x;
+                const double dy = sy[j] - y;
+                const double dz = sz[j] - z;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz));
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
+        }
+        // Inactive lanes also participate before the next tile overwrites it.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = velocities[i] + DT * fx;
+        const double vy = velocities[stride + i] + DT * fy;
+        const double vz = velocities[2 * stride + i] + DT * fz;
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // Every force reads the old positions; the next step reads this buffer.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, x);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, y);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, z);
+    }
 }

+        if (n == 0) return;
+        std::vector<double> final(6 * stride);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data(), positions, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data() + 3 * stride, velocities, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading velocities");
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(final[i], final[stride + i], final[2 * stride + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(final[3 * stride + i], final[4 * stride + i], final[5 * stride + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    double totalEnergy() const {
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        double* deviceEnergies = nullptr;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double)), "allocating energy output");
+        energyPerBody<<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, velocities, deviceEnergies, stride, n);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching energy kernel");
+        std::vector<double> energies(n);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "computing energy");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(energies.data(), deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading energies");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceEnergies), "freeing energy output");
+        return kahan_sum(energies);
+    }
+
+private:
+    void launchStep() {
+        if (n < 8192) {
+            advanceBodies<32><<<(size_t(n) + 31) / 32, 32, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<128><<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching body kernel");
+        std::swap(positions, nextPositions);
+    }
+
+    int n;
+    size_t stride;
+    double* storage = nullptr;
+    double* positions = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions = nullptr;
+    double* velocities = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executableGraph = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps = 0;
+};

The first CUDA build matches the original’s default-run result hash exactly. I’ve also moved energy calculation to the GPU and added bounded CUDA graph batches to reduce launch overhead. Next I’ll check tile boundaries and longer runs, then tune work distribution for different body counts.

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {

-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+constexpr int TILE_SIZE = 256;
+
+// Explicit rounding keeps the velocity update separate from integration.
+// Fused operations elsewhere preserve the original optimized CPU arithmetic.
+__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(double dx, double dy, double dz) {
+    return __fma_rn(dz, dz, __fma_rn(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;
+}
+
+template<int BLOCK_SIZE>
+__global__ void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positions,
+                              double* __restrict__ nextPositions,
+                              double* __restrict__ velocities,
+                              size_t stride, int n) {
+    __shared__ double sx[TILE_SIZE], sy[TILE_SIZE], sz[TILE_SIZE];
+    const size_t i = size_t(blockIdx.x) * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;
+    const bool active = i < size_t(n);
+    const double x = active ? positions[i] : 0.0;
+    const double y = active ? positions[stride + i] : 0.0;
+    const double z = active ? positions[2 * stride + i] : 0.0;
+    double fx = 0.0, fy = 0.0, fz = 0.0;
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < size_t(n); base += TILE_SIZE) {
+        const int count = int(size_t(n) - base < TILE_SIZE ? size_t(n) - base : TILE_SIZE);
+        for (int k = threadIdx.x; k < count; k += BLOCK_SIZE) {
+            sx[k] = positions[base + k];
+            sy[k] = positions[stride + base + k];
+            sz[k] = positions[2 * stride + base + k];
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (active) {
+            // Keep the j accumulation order, including the zero self force.
+            #pragma unroll 8
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const double dx = sx[j] - x;
+                const double dy = sy[j] - y;
+                const double dz = sz[j] - z;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz));
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+                fx = __fma_rn(dx, invDist3, fx);
+                fy = __fma_rn(dy, invDist3, fy);
+                fz = __fma_rn(dz, invDist3, fz);
+            }
+        }
+        // Inactive lanes also participate before the next tile overwrites it.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    if (active) {
+        const double vx = velocities[i] + DT * fx;
+        const double vy = velocities[stride + i] + DT * fy;
+        const double vz = velocities[2 * stride + i] + DT * fz;
+        velocities[i] = vx;
+        velocities[stride + i] = vy;
+        velocities[2 * stride + i] = vz;
+        // Every force reads the old positions; the next step reads this buffer.
+        nextPositions[i] = __fma_rn(vx, DT, x);
+        nextPositions[stride + i] = __fma_rn(vy, DT, y);
+        nextPositions[2 * stride + i] = __fma_rn(vz, DT, z);
+    }
 }

+class CudaSimulation {
+public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, int steps)
+        : n(int(bodies.size())), stride((size_t(n) + 31) / 32 * 32) {
+        // A GPU is required even for an empty simulation; there is no CPU path.
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "initializing the CUDA device");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking), "creating stream");
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&storage, 9 * stride * sizeof(double)), "allocating body state");
+        positions = storage;
+        velocities = storage + 3 * stride;
+        nextPositions = storage + 6 * stride;
+        std::vector<double> initial(6 * stride, 0.0);
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            initial[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            initial[stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            initial[2 * stride + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            initial[3 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            initial[4 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            initial[5 * stride + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(storage, initial.data(), initial.size() * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "uploading body state");
+        // An even graph length leaves the position buffers in their initial roles.
+        // Bounded batches avoid building a graph proportional to the step count.
+        graphSteps = steps > 1 ? (steps < 32 ? steps / 2 * 2 : 32) : 0;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal), "starting graph capture");
+            for (int step = 0; step < graphSteps; ++step) launchStep();
+            cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &graph), "ending graph capture");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&executableGraph, graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0), "instantiating graph");
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphDestroy(graph), "destroying captured graph");
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        if (executableGraph) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executableGraph);
+        if (storage) cudaFree(storage);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    void run(int steps) {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        int remaining = steps;
+        if (graphSteps) {
+            for (; remaining >= graphSteps; remaining -= graphSteps)
+                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(executableGraph, stream), "launching simulation graph");
+        }
+        for (; remaining > 0; --remaining) launchStep();
+        // Include completion of all GPU work in the reported simulation time.
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "running simulation");
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) const {
+        if (n == 0) return;
+        std::vector<double> final(6 * stride);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data(), positions, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading positions");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(final.data() + 3 * stride, velocities, 3 * stride * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading velocities");
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(final[i], final[stride + i], final[2 * stride + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(final[3 * stride + i], final[4 * stride + i], final[5 * stride + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    double totalEnergy() const {
+        if (n == 0) return 0.0;
+        double* deviceEnergies = nullptr;
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double)), "allocating energy output");
+        energyPerBody<<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, velocities, deviceEnergies, stride, n);
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching energy kernel");
+        std::vector<double> energies(n);
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream), "computing energy");
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpy(energies.data(), deviceEnergies, size_t(n) * sizeof(double),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "downloading energies");
+        checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceEnergies), "freeing energy output");
+        return kahan_sum(energies);
+    }
+
+private:
+    void launchStep() {
+        if (n < 8192) {
+            advanceBodies<32><<<(size_t(n) + 31) / 32, 32, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        } else {
+            advanceBodies<128><<<(size_t(n) + 127) / 128, 128, 0, stream>>>(positions, nextPositions, velocities, stride, n);
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launching body kernel");
+        std::swap(positions, nextPositions);
+    }
+
+    int n;
+    size_t stride;
+    double* storage = nullptr;
+    double* positions = nullptr;
+    double* nextPositions = nullptr;
+    double* velocities = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executableGraph = nullptr;
+    int graphSteps = 0;
+};

     for (const auto& body : bodies) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error in %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
